**✦ Ask is a conversation: it checks the script (duplicate characters, props that aren't props, locations), analyses it, asks when unsure, and says what it did**
- **Still wrong (read first):** never run with the real local model: the model path is tested with canned replies, so how well Qwen3.5 4B picks tools from this prompt (one JSON turn, the project summary cut at 2400 characters, the last 8 turns of history) is unmeasured; a 4B model may pick the wrong check or skip one, and then the answer only covers what it called (the card's *What I did* lists exactly what ran). Not clicked through in a browser. The checks are the App's own word work, with or without the model: no appearance clashes (the model isn't asked to read the descriptions), beats are each quarter's opening action line, not a reading of the story, the props list is common words only, and an age clash is only seen where a character is introduced as `NAME (30s)`. "The same for scene 3" works without the model only when the last turn was a check. A merge renames cues and the ALL-CAPS name in action, not a lower-case "Mara" in prose. Fixes apply only from a card's **Apply** (never by the model), and their **Undo** is the script's own (not the top bar's ⌘Z). With the model on, each non-recipe, non-set-order message costs one model round trip (up to 15 s) before any plan.
- **Root cause:** Ask only knew two things: a how-to question matched against recipes, and everything else went to the Director's set planner. "Double-check the characters… check the props… read the script and analyse it" has no question mark and no recipe, so the planner turned it into a set change.
- **What** ([web/agent/assistant.mjs](web/agent/assistant.mjs), [web/agent/ask.mjs](web/agent/ask.mjs), [web/agent/directorpanel.mjs](web/agent/directorpanel.mjs)): a `Conversation` keeps the chat's history and sends a request to the Director's model (local Qwen, or the Ollama / API model in ⚙) with what the project holds (script, scenes, cast, locations, props, shots), where you are, and a tool list: `analyze_script`, `check_cast`, `check_props`, `check_locations`, `plan_stage` (handed to the planner: plan, Apply, Undo this), `show_me` (a recipe), `add_note` (the Director's Book). It may ask one clarifying question instead. Each check's card shows what it found, **What I did**, its limits, and **Apply N fixes** (merge characters, rename a location, move or drop a prop; kept on the scene as `breakdown_fix` so a re-read keeps it, [web/script/breakdown.mjs](web/script/breakdown.mjs) `applyBreakdownFixes`) with **Undo**. An unknown tool is said ("I can't do … yet, the nearest I can do…"). With no model (the try-it demo, no GPU, the GPU busy rendering, the Director on the rules) it says so and runs the same checks from the words of the request, asks "What should I check?" with four choices when the request is vague, and leaves set orders to the planner. **+** in the chat header starts a new chat; an error has **Retry**. How-to questions keep their instant recipe answer; set orders ("make it rain") still go straight to the planner.

**Projects: a Save / Open / New pill with autosave, and Collect project (an organised, relinkable copy of everything)**

A Save from the pill writes the file but does not clear the "modified" dot on ComfyUI's own workflow tab. The pill serializes the graph every 3 s to spot changes, which costs more on a very large stage. The last-moment autosave on tab close may not land before the tab goes. Collect only finds the files the node still names: `ss_renders` keeps the newest render of each kind per shot, so older drafts and finals are not collected. The cut exports come from the live scene's editor only. Voice samples are matched by the Character Voice name set in Cast. Downloading a big collected zip goes through ComfyUI's own server and has not been timed.
- **What:** a project pill in the App's top row ([web/project/projectbar.mjs](web/project/projectbar.mjs)) with Save, Save As, Open, Open recent, New project (the Soundstage_App template), Restore autosave and Autosave on/off. Save writes `workflows/<name>.json` (same as Workflow → Save) and `projects/<name>/project.json`. Autosave writes `projects/<name>/autosave/<stamp>.json` 4 s after a change and at least every 60 s while changing, keeps 20, and names an unnamed project `Untitled <date> <time>`. `beforeunload` asks when there are changes since the last Save. The pure half (names, paths, rotation, the clock, the pill text) is [web/project/store.mjs](web/project/store.mjs). The project name rides in `extra.ss_project`; old workflows without it open as before. Nothing is written into `output/` except by Collect.
- **Collect project** (the pill and a card in ⑦ Deliver, [soundstage/project/collect.py](soundstage/project/collect.py), [routes.py](soundstage/project/routes.py)): copies every file reference in the stage node's `ss_*` properties into `output/soundstage_projects/<Project>/`, as `scenes/S01/shots/S01_010/{clay,frames,refs,takes/draft,takes/final,mattes,depth,camera,sound,layerpack}`, `cast/<character>/` (refs, cast sheets, voice samples), `locations/<set>/`, `script/`, and `cut/` (timeline JSON plus the existing EDL / XML / FCPXML / OTIO exporters). Files are named `<shot>_<kind>_<take>.<ext>`. The shot's newest Layer Pack is included. It adds `project.json` (with the take list, the chosen take and a relink table), `README.txt`, and `MISSING.txt` for files that are not there. It downloads as a zip. **Open collected project** opens one from the server or from an uploaded zip, with every reference relinked to the copies. Names are cleaned to one path segment. Sources must stay inside output, input, temp or the voices folder, and destinations inside the project folder. A zip entry outside its folder, or a symlink, rejects the whole zip. See [docs/projects.md](docs/projects.md).

**✦ Ask always answers (or says why) within ~20 s, Stop for a stuck question, "lock it in" applies the plan; ④ Frame takes any LoRA, one at a time**
- **Still wrong (read first):** not run in the live App or on a pod: checked with Node tests that mount the real chat on a small fake DOM (no browser) and pytest; no GPU, no LoRA ever loaded. The 20 s limit is the whole turn, so a cold local Qwen (first load on the GPU queue) now gives up and plans with the rules, saying "model unavailable (no answer after N s)"; the model job is taken off the queue only if it hasn't started (a running one finishes and warms the model for the next turn). Stop gives up on waiting; it can't take back a plan already being applied (that isn't stoppable: your new words go back in the box instead). The LoRA lists sort by name or folder only ("klein" / "flux2" / "flux3", "krea", "ltx"...), so a LoRA named after a character lands under "Other" with a "not sure" warning even when it is a Klein LoRA. The server can only tell a wrong family from the file's own header (kohya / modelspec metadata, SD / SDXL key names, LTX audio keys): a FLUX.1 or Krea LoRA without metadata on Klein still runs and is quietly ignored by ComfyUI ("lora key not loaded" in its log). **Krea 2 + depth · strict** paints without an add-on (that graph has no LoRA slot), and says so. Private / downloads / NDA / clients folders stay out of the list (the 2026-11-02 screen-share fix), so it is "any LoRA" except those, unless one is already chosen. The lean `Soundstage_App.json` has no Extra LoRA slot in ⑤ (the full App does): the select says so and is disabled.
- **Root causes (Ask, [web/agent/directorpanel.mjs](web/agent/directorpanel.mjs) `send`, [ask.mjs](web/agent/ask.mjs), [director.mjs](web/agent/director.mjs)):** (1) no time limit on the answer hook or the Director routes (the local model had 90 s, `/director/prompt` and `/director/plan` none), and the question only appeared in the chat once its answer came back: a silent hang. (2) While a request hung, every new question got "One moment: still on the last one." and its words were thrown away (the box was already cleared): the old question "stuck". (3) "lock it in", "looks good", "ship it" weren't a yes, and any other words while a plan waits discard it and plan again: the plan could not be locked in by saying so. (4) A free-form answer that failed was swallowed (`catch { return null }`), no word in the chat. (5) Whisper: letting go of the mic before the browser granted it left the mic recording and blocked the next press.
- **What:** the question shows at once with *Thinking…* and a **Stop** (also Esc, or "stop"); the answer and the plan each run under `ASK_TIMEOUT_MS` (20 s; the routes 8 s; the free answer 15 s) through `withTimeout` and an AbortController that reaches the local model's queue job, Ollama and the API fetch ([web/agent/agent.mjs](web/agent/agent.mjs)); a newer question stops a stuck one; a late answer from a stopped one is dropped; an error is said in the chat; the box is empty and focused after Enter or Send. `YES_RE` takes "lock it (in)", "looks good", "ship it", "do that", "perfect", "yes please"... A Whisper transcription is limited to 20 s too.
- **LoRA:** ④ Frame's *Style or character add-on* lists every LoRA under **Matches this model** (the model the frame node paints with: FLUX 3 Klein, or Krea 2 Turbo under the Strict depth lock) and **Other LoRAs (may not work with this model)**, with a short warning under the select for those ([web/pipeline/loras.mjs](web/pipeline/loras.mjs)); the list is regrouped when the model or the lock changes. The router checks the pick before building the graph ([soundstage/router/lora_check.py](soundstage/router/lora_check.py), called from `_Router._prepare`): gone from models/loras, or made for another family by its own header, stops with a sentence. ⑤ Motion → Advanced (LTX) → Extra LoRA (node 9101) worked end to end in the full App; it now lists LTX LoRAs first and refreshes (it filled once and kept a stale list), keeps a shot's pick listed if the file is gone, and `SoundstageLTXLoRA` stops on a LoRA whose header names another model ([soundstage/nodes_quality.py](soundstage/nodes_quality.py)).

- **Fix this object / Remove (not in the video) / Add a missed object** ([soundstage/video2stage/fixobject.py](soundstage/video2stage/fixobject.py), right-click over the view, the Identical? bar): SAM 3.1 tracks only that object from a click on a source frame (or its projection), forward and back over the samples; the clearest frames are ranked (size, whole, typical shape, sharpness) and the best one is rebuilt alone through Photo → 3D (TripoSplat → SplatToMesh, the SAM mask as alpha); it keeps the solve's place, turn and size and swaps in as the next version, with Undo. Without TripoSplat only its colour is re-read. Both jobs show their steps on the job card and name their GPU prompt in Activity; the try-it demo gives the full-install note.

**20:55 PST · ④ Frame: the still prompt names only what the shot camera sees at frame 0**
- **Still wrong (read first):** the Look description is filtered by word, not by meaning. A sentence is dropped when it names an off-frame cast member ("police", or any car / person word when no vehicle / no person is in frame), unless it is a negation ("no cars"); a description that names an off-frame car only by brand or a nickname keeps it. Occlusion is only checked against buildings and houses (both the head and the middle of a figure behind one), not other figures, trees or cars. A mocap clay's facing is its root yaw at frame 0 (Video → Stage's world track); a chest or head turned against the hips is not read. On the dancer the new wording turned her body three-quarter to screen-left as the clay has it, but Klein still turned her face to the lens. "The camera circles her slowly" (a motion clause in the Look description) still reaches the still. Checked with Klein stills on pod 1 for 2 shots, no motion renders.
- **Root cause** ([soundstage/look/compile.py](soundstage/look/compile.py) `compile_still`): frame 1 used the frame-0 frustum for the blocking but not for three other inputs: the traffic lock ("the only vehicles are a police car") counted every car on stage, the swimmers' kit words counted every diver on stage, and the Look description went in whole. People's facing had three bands, so a dancer turned ~52° from the lens read "facing the camera".
- **Fix:** `S.traffic_lock(scene, visible)` names only the cars (and boats) in frame at frame 0, else "a deserted street with empty sidewalks and an empty road"; `SW.still_words` / `negative_words` take the ids in frame; `frame1_safe(text, absent_words(scene, f1))` drops the description's sentences about cast that is off frame at frame 0. The motion prompt keeps all of them. `frame_facts` also hides a figure or car behind a building ([scene.py](soundstage/look/scene.py) `_occluded_by_props`). People face in five bands from the clay at frame 0 (`_facing_words`; a Video → Stage take from its `mocap.world.yaw`): facing the camera, three-quarter toward it, profile, three-quarter from behind, and "seen from behind, with their back to the camera" (plus "facing the camera, face toward the lens" in the still's negative).
- **Tests:** [tests/test_frame1_visibility.py](tests/test_frame1_visibility.py) (new, 8): the car up the street at frame 0 is out of the still and in the motion prompt, a car in frame is still named, a diver below frame loses his kit in the still only, negations stay, back / profile / three-quarter / front, the mocap world yaw, a man behind a building. `test_look_scene` now expects "seen from behind" for the default scene's walkers. **20:50 PST · Drive takes over from keys (a car keyed mid J-turn drives instead of sticking), kept takes are auto-corrected and fitted with far fewer keys, and ⌂ Back to the set / ↶ Last view on the viewport**
- **Still wrong (read first):** checked in Node tests and headless Chrome on a local build of the try-it page (swiftshader), not in the live App or on the pod; the ⌂ / ↶ buttons sit under the App's view badge by measurement, not by a screenshot of the App. The key fit picks which recorded frames become keys but does not solve their values (the camera's reducer does), so a fast weaving car still needs a key every 6-9 frames (a minute at 43 km/h: 1,440 frames -> about 150 keys); people, horses and steady drives come out much lower. Phone camera takes were already fitted to 1.2 cm by movefit/stabilize.mjs: they now report "N frames -> K keys", but have no raw-take Undo and no new reducer. Foot sliding is left to the gait planner (feet are planted from the path, not corrected after it). Props, thrown things and boats' paddle strokes are not auto-corrected. Undo to the raw take covers only the last kept take, and only until the next take is kept. A car driven into a wall at an angle still stops against it rather than sliding along it. Home view frames everything, so on a set with a large floor it can be far out.
- **Root cause** ([web/stage/drive.mjs](web/stage/drive.mjs), [web/stage/vehsim.mjs](web/stage/vehsim.mjs)): the stage already skipped a driven car's path keys, but (1) a keyed stunt pose often overlaps a kerb, wall or parked car (the stunt simulation doesn't collide), and `LiveCar.bump` took 30 % of the speed and half the yaw rate on every contact, even driving away from the wall, so a car started there could not get out: in the repro, W held for 2 s moved it 0.2 m; (2) keys still leaked into a driven object: the stunt's keyed tyre smoke kept pouring where the keys had the car, and Pose Studio keys and mocap still posed a driven person; (3) the keyed roll and pitch snapped to level on the first frame; (4) recording sampled 24 fps frames from a 60 Hz sim (2 or 3 steps apart), so a steady drive was keyed 40 % fast, slow, fast, and a slow screen frame sampled one state twice and then jumped.
- **Takeover:** `DriveMode.owns(id)`; the stage skips Pose Studio keys and mocap for a driven object ([web/stage/stage.mjs](web/stage/stage.mjs)), and the car's keyed smoke source is dropped while it is driven. `_settle`: the yaw is kept from the full rotation (also for a tilted or flipped keyed pose), the keyed lean, roll and pitch ease out over `SETTLE_S` (0.3 s), feet and hooves go onto the ground, and the body is pushed clear of what the keyed pose overlapped. HUD note "Took over from keys: Esc to leave". `bump` only takes speed off on a real hit. Esc without a take restores the keys exactly (unchanged).
- **Recording:** a whole number of sim steps per shot frame (72 Hz at 24 fps), and frames are posed and sampled inside the sim steps (`_recStep`).
- **Auto-correct and key fit** ([web/stage/takefix.mjs](web/stage/takefix.mjs), new): `limitsFor` (the live car's grip, top gear and roll gradient; a person's or a horse's top speed, acceleration and turn rate), `correctTrack` (dropped frames, teleports, out-and-back spikes, top speed, yaw spikes and turn rate, roll and pitch, ground), `reduceKeys` (replays keys as the stage does, splits the worst segment until every frame is within 2 cm and 1°, keeps mode changes and holds). `applyTake(..., {reduce})`; a take under review is already the corrected one. The HUD toast (`takeReport`: "Smoothed 3 spikes, settled roll · 1,440 keys → 38") has **Undo** (`useRaw`: the raw take, a key every 2 frames). The phone viewfinder and the desk report "frames → keys" ([web/vcam/viewfinder.mjs](web/vcam/viewfinder.mjs), [web/vcam/desk.mjs](web/vcam/desk.mjs)).
- **Lost camera** ([web/stage/viewnav.mjs](web/stage/viewnav.mjs), new): **⌂ Back to the set** (.) flies the free view to a home view that fits the set's boxes and objects in 0.4 s (the shot camera is not moved); **↶ Last view** (,) steps back through the last 10 settled views (rested 0.5 s, or marked before Drive, Back to the set, F or a compass jump); a **Lost? Back to the set** chip when the free view is more than 3 set radii out or inside a set box. Both keys are on the shortcut sheet ([web/ui/shortcuts.mjs](web/ui/shortcuts.mjs)).
- **Tests:** [tests/js/drive_takeover.test.mjs](tests/js/drive_takeover.test.mjs) (new, 6: keys suspended and restored, settle, pushed clear of a wall, a person planted, a clean car and a clean walk pass unchanged, a spike / a teleport / top speed / roll / ground, the key fit within tolerance), [tests/js/viewnav.test.mjs](tests/js/viewnav.test.mjs) (new, 4). JS 1178: 1176 passed, 2 failed (the two try-it publish checks in tryit_edges.test.mjs, which fail on main too); Python 1625 passed (25 skipped). Headless: before / after screenshots and logs in share/QA/drive_keyed_override/. Docs: [TUTORIAL: Drive mode](docs/TUTORIAL.md#drive-mode-control-anything-like-a-video-game-and-record-it).

A queued job of yours taken off the queue (its own Cancel) stayed "queued" forever; jobs of yours that leave the queue unseen are now looked up by `GET /history/{prompt_id}` (never the full `/history`) and their status messages replay, so a missed error still names its node. Every progress event rebuilt the panel, so a Cancel click during sampling landed on a replaced button and was lost; rebuilds are now at most 4 a second, never while a button is held, and keep open Details and scroll. Each Stage node mounted its own registry, listeners and 4 s poll; now one per page. "Frame interpolation" and "Soundtrack" were filed as Frame and Video → 3D. Past its average a job read "about 0 s left" forever; it now says "longer than usual" or the sampler's rate. "another user" is now "another session".
- **Node-level progress** (Mike: "which node it's on in the background … it's going through the KSampler now, or it's doing the depth map now"): the prompt is read once at queue time from `api.fetchApi` (`watchQueueing`), so a node id from the events gets its class_type and title. `STAGES` maps the class types in the App's graphs (45 rules) to stage names (Sampling, Upscaling ×2, Depth map, Pose (DWPose), Masking (SAM 3), Decoding video (VAE), Encoding prompt (Gemma), Loading model, Saving MP4, 3D stage, Layer pack…), class_type as the fallback, a set title wins; samplers and latent upscalers carry ": stage N" when a prompt has more than one sampling stage, and the ×2 comes from the upscale model's file name. Running jobs show the stage, `step 8/12` with an inner bar, `node 7 of 11 · 2 cached`, elapsed and time left; **Details** lists nodes as they run with their times, cached ones folded, and **Node types (class and id)** switches to `KSampler #12` (kept in this browser). `progress_state` (newer ComfyUI) and `execution_cached` are followed. Failures read *Failed at Decoding video (VAE) (VAEDecodeTiled #16)*, the plain words, then ComfyUI's first line; interrupts read *Cancelled at Depth map*. The pill leads with the stage and steps and has a 2 px progress bar.

A **Deleted N frames · Undo** toast stays 5 s (candidates are not on the stage's undo stack). The shot's chosen frame is never deleted without an answer in a red bar over the list (**Delete it** / **Keep it**; Clear all offers **Keep the chosen one, clear N**), no browser dialog; deleting it also drops the shot's saved first frame (`forgetRender`, [web/soundstage.js](web/soundstage.js)), and Undo puts both back. Neither one steers the next generation, and the tooltip says so.
- **B. ③ Set → ④ Frame:** **④ Make the first frame →** at the end of ③ Set's time bar, in the outlined style of **⑤ Animate this frame →** ([web/ui/theme.mjs](web/ui/theme.mjs)). It opens ④ Frame (which re-renders the clay and keeps its image model, FLUX 3 Klein unless you picked another) and generates for the active shot; Paint Studio stays closed. With a frame already there it reads **④ Remake the first frame →**, and the old frame stays in the list, still chosen until you pick a new one. A shot that is already painting is not queued twice.

Try-it demo** ([tools/tryit/shell/provenance.mjs](tools/tryit/shell/provenance.mjs), [demo.mjs](tools/tryit/shell/demo.mjs), [scripts/api.js](tools/tryit/shell/scripts/api.js)): the shell remembers each scene's stage as it loaded (cast, moves, every setup's camera, set and light; not the playhead or the selection). **15:50 PST · World collisions, edge cases: a queue of touching cars is no longer a wall, things placed inside each other no longer fly apart when hit, and a ram after a bake connects**
- **Still wrong (read first):** checked headless in Node only, like the earlier world-collision entries. Things placed more than 2 cm inside each other are now eased apart slowly after a hit (about 0.5 m/s, with no speed from it), so a car parked 30 cm into a wall slides out of it over a few frames; the consequence check still reports the overlap at 0.0 s after the bake. The solver's contact depth for deep hull overlaps is still not the real depth (it reads about 1 cm), which is why overlaps are eased rather than resolved. `rerouteKeys` adds the swerve key only (its comment said it also eased back a second later; it never did). Clear simulation leaves `physics.fps`/`frames` behind (harmless).
- **Touching queue** ([physics.mjs](web/stage/physics.mjs) `heldHits`): a car shunted into a parked car it touched (0 to 1 cm) stopped dead, and so did the car that hit it: a held body is immovable in the solve, and the hit was gone before its hold was checked. With a 2 cm gap it worked. When the solve takes more than 0.5 m/s out of something against a held body, the substep is now solved again with the hold checked at that speed first. 15 m/s into a bumper-to-bumper pair: the front car used to stay put; now it is shoved about 3 m (2.2 m with a 10 cm gap).
- **Placed inside each other** ([physics.mjs](web/stage/physics.mjs) ghost pairs): a car parked 20-30 cm into a wall, another car, a lamp or a person, hit from behind at 15 m/s, flew back at 20 to 40 m/s with its hitter (the solver pushed the overlap out as speed). Such pairs now stay exactly put until hit, then ease apart with no speed from it and still stop each other going further in (no hit events, dents or "pressed" takeovers from the overlap).
- **Director `ram` / `drive` on a baked car** ([director.mjs](web/agent/director.mjs)): the new keys were taken as edits of the old bake and merged into the car's old authored keys, so a middle key of its old path stayed and the ram missed ("They never touched"). The car is now un-baked first.

- **Rigs** ([web/stage/sfx.mjs](web/stage/sfx.mjs), [web/stage/sfxstage.mjs](web/stage/sfxstage.mjs)): actors of kind `sfx_*` with a cue: **at a time**, **on contact** (the first hard hit on what it watches, or anything reaching it: a car through breakaway glass), **after another cue** (a chain with a delay) or **manual**: 💥 Fire / K at the playhead, during playback too, or K during Drive-mode ● REC ([drive.mjs](web/stage/drive.mjs)), written down as the rig's key (one Undo step). Markers show while blocking and never in a capture, Depth or the ID matte; a breakaway pane is a real prop until it shatters.
- **The set reacts** ([physics.mjs](web/stage/physics.mjs) `simulate(…, { blasts })`): an explosion gives every body a radial push with falloff (full in a 2 m core, then inverse-square to zero at its reach), by its exposed face and mass, at the side facing the charge: crates fly, a parked car rocks (power 1) or rolls / lands on its roof (1.5-2), a lamp post falls, a person is knocked down, a crate bursts (the blast loads the fracture network). Fire spreads along wood, cardboard and fabric props; scorch marks; the practical light (the fireball's flash, firelight, a flare's red glow); camera shake when the shockwave reaches the camera. A contact cue re-bakes (from the authored keys) until no new cue fires.
- **On the actor:** body squibs and blood packs on the wardrobe (chest, stomach, shoulders, back, arms, thighs) and a stunt-knife gag: a puff of fabric and stage blood, the reaction by where it lands (a chest hit snaps back and falls, an arm flinches and stays up, a thigh buckles to a knee, a stab doubles them over), and a stain and a trickle that stay. Film level only: stage blood, no real weapons or devices.
- **Reversible:** scrubbing before a cue shows the untouched originals (everything is a function of the frame); **Reset to before** (on the rig, or on anything it touched) disarms it and puts every touched object back byte for byte (one Undo step); changing a setting re-bakes from the untouched scene; **A / B** swaps the previous and the new run ([sfxpanel.mjs](web/stage/sfxpanel.mjs) in the Physics panel).
- **Clay and Color:** fireball, flames and flare puffs are self-lit and cool to smoke; smoke columns, coloured smoke, stage blood, charred debris and glass go through the one particle system ([particles.mjs](web/stage/particles.mjs), [secondary.mjs](web/stage/secondary.mjs)); stains, scorch and the pane keep their look under Clay's override.

**13:50 PST · Tests: the fracture network's bake-time check no longer flakes inside the full suite**
- **Still wrong (read first):** the 4 s CPU target now runs only on request (`SOUNDSTAGE_PERF_STRICT=1`, file run alone), so a slow drift between 4 and 12 s would only show up there or as a ratio jump.
- **Why it flaked:** `node --test tests/js/*.test.mjs` runs the files in parallel processes. Measured on Windows: the same crate crash costs 1.3 s rigid / 1.5 s network CPU alone, but 3.1 s / 3.5 s inside the full suite, so the absolute 4 s limit broke (Mike saw 4.0-4.4 s on the Mac) while the network-vs-rigid ratio stayed at 1.14. On Windows `loadavg()` is always 0, so the idle gate never helped there.
- **What:** [tests/js/fracture_network.test.mjs](tests/js/fracture_network.test.mjs) keeps the same-process ratio (network at most 4.5x the rigid bake) on every run, adds a 12 s ceiling, and keeps the 4 s target behind `SOUNDSTAGE_PERF_STRICT=1`.

**13:15 PST · Bug hunt over the last 48 h of main (PR #5): 21 fixes, a lens-key refill fix, and the phone bridge only pipes a WebSocket that ComfyUI actually accepted**
- **Still wrong (read first):** every fix is checked by unit and end-to-end tests on Windows only; none was clicked through in the live App, run on the pod (not deployed) or tried on a real phone. The bridge fix is tested against a fake ComfyUI and a local aiohttp server, not over Wi-Fi with the SSH tunnel. The bridge now refuses chunked request bodies (411): the phone pages always send a Content-Length, but a client that streams an upload is turned away. The suites still show main's known failures (Python 26, JS 3, all present on main before this branch); none is new.
- **Phone bridge** ([tools/vcam_bridge.py](tools/vcam_bridge.py)): a paired phone could reach any ComfyUI route. It sent `GET /soundstage/vcam/ws` with `Upgrade: websocket` and a broken handshake; ComfyUI answered 400 and kept the connection open, but the bridge had already switched to raw piping, so a `GET /system_stats` or `POST /prompt` pipelined behind it went straight through (reproduced against aiohttp). The bridge now reads ComfyUI's answer and pipes only after `101` with `Upgrade: websocket`. Any other answer goes back as one response and the connection closes. Plain requests forward only their `Content-Length` body, so nothing rides behind an allowed request either. Since f83e5e5, earlier on this branch, only `/soundstage/vcam/ws` may upgrade at all. Tests: [tests/test_vcam_bridge.py](tests/test_vcam_bridge.py) (new, 16 tests; 9 fail on the old bridge, including the smuggled request). Docs: [docs/vcam.md](docs/vcam.md).

- **Video → 3D / Stage:** Cancel stops the server job and takes its own GPU prompt off the queue; a sofa, box or plant in the shot breakdown is placed again; an imported world's hidden ground stays hidden after a set rebuild; `Stage.dispose` stops the rotate tools' window listeners and the Parts panels' polls.

**12:10 PST · World collisions: everything collides in the bake, keyed cars crash instead of passing through, and the Director checks consequences before a render**
- **Still wrong (read first):** checked headless in Node only (the real Stage scene graph, no WebGL, built cars because model files don't load headless); not watched in a browser or rendered on the pod. After the hit a car is one rigid body: its crush is a dent plus a logged depth, nothing folds or comes off, and its wheels don't spin or steer. People are ragdoll-lite (one rigid body that topples) and keep playing the walk cycle while they slide. A driver in a car that rolls turns with it but doesn't roll with it. A car stops against a solid wall in one substep (a real crush takes ~0.1 s). Only the set's lamp posts are knock-down set pieces (street, plaza); facades and planters are fixed. The takeover marker is in the bake message and the Physics panel, not yet a tick on the timeline.
- **What:** Simulate & bake treats the set as a real place ([web/stage/world.mjs](web/stage/world.mjs), [docs/world.md](docs/world.md)). Cars, people, street lamps, benches, hydrants, trees and walls are bodies with real masses, anchoring and crush ([physics.mjs](web/stage/physics.mjs) `takeover`, `anchor`, `aniso`, `crush`). A keyed car keeps its keys until its first real hit, then the physics takes over by mass and speed: through another car it is a crash (momentum shared, spin, roll or stop), into a lamp it knocks it down (breakaway base), into a solid wall it stops and crumples, through a weak (wooden) wall it breaks a hole, and a person in the way is knocked over. A prop nobody set up rests until touched, and a stack comes down when what holds it goes. Each takeover is marked ("Police car: physics took over at f 42 (1.8 s), hit the red car at 44 km/h"). **Keep keyed** holds one actor on its keys, and **Everything collides** (on by default) switches it all off ([physpanel.mjs](web/stage/physpanel.mjs)).
- **Reversible and fluid:** the bake is deterministic and scrubbing back plays exactly the same frames. One Undo restores the scene from before the bake. Editing a key or dragging anything in a baked scene re-bakes it 0.45 s after you stop, and the edit and its re-bake are one Undo step. An edit on a baked object goes into its authored keys ([stage.mjs](web/stage/stage.mjs) `_worldPoke`, world.mjs `mergeEdits`).
- **Consequence check (Director):** *"check for collisions"*, and automatically before every render, sweeps the shot on the same convex shapes the bake collides and says what passes through what in plain words: *"The police car passes through the red car at 2.1 s: crash them, or reroute?"*, with **Crash them** / **Reroute** chips. An unbaked scene is baked first, and anything still passing through stops the render. New tools `ram` (*"the police car rams the red car into the boxes"*: the speed is planned by momentum so the red car reaches the boxes, then the bake plays it out), `check`, `reroute` and `keep_keyed` ([director.py](soundstage/agent/director.py), [director.mjs](web/agent/director.mjs)).
- **Numbers (from the tests):** rear-end at 54 km/h, 1.9 t into 1.45 t: 99.7 % of the momentum kept through the hit, restitution about 0.1, the red car leaves at 9.35 m/s (8.51 if perfectly plastic). Rigid wall at 43 km/h: crush 0.446 m against the linear-crush answer of 0.457 m. Breakaway lamp: the car loses 0.89 m/s (AASHTO: 1-3). Weak wall: a car at 12 m/s breaks through and leaves at 7.2 m/s, and at 1.2 m/s the wall holds. Kerb trip: upright at 0.8× the analytic critical sliding velocity (9.4 m/s), on its roof at 1.5× (the sim's own threshold falls between 1.1× and 1.25×). Mike's demo (police car through 6 crates and the red car) bakes in 2.7 s headless, with nothing interpenetrating afterwards.

**08:15 PST · Motion graphics on the Cut timeline: nine templates on a V3 lane, each with its own designed sounds on A2**
- **Still wrong (read first):** the pod's ComfyUI has **not been restarted**, so its running server has the old render and no `/soundstage/cut/gfx_sfx` route: in the App the graphics edit and preview, but a render ignores them and their sounds don't load (the Cut says so once) until a restart. The render path was checked on the pod with the new code in a separate Python process: a 30 s 1080p cut with all nine kinds of graphic took 297 s on the busy pod CPU (about 10× real time). Nobody has listened to the sounds yet: they pass an AudioSet classifier check (no Speech, Singing or Animal above 0.3 in any of the 32 files), which says nothing about taste; the classifier calls the whooshes "Whip". Type is Space Grotesk, the App's own face, not the spec's Inter Tight (not vendored). Not built: the device plane break and through-the-screen move, the clapper-logo animation (it needs Mike's OK), tracked tags, counters, real 9:16 layouts (a 9:16 render fits the 16:9 layout to the width) and NLE export of graphics. The viewer preview can lag a frame on window clips because they seek their own video.
- **What:** **+ Graphic…** in the Cut editor puts a motion graphic on a new **V3 Graphics** lane at the playhead ([web/cut/gfx.mjs](web/cut/gfx.mjs), [web/cut/cut.mjs](web/cut/cut.mjs)). The nine templates follow share/Commercial/DESIGN_SYSTEM.md v1.1 (grid, type scale, colours, easing curves, anticipation and overshoot, springs, layouts): **Title**, **Kinetic type** (a slam, then one word per beat), **Lower third**, **Callout zoom** (zoom into a box, ring, chip, optional cursor click), **PiP corner**, **Dual PiP** (side by side, stagger, or a window plus a still card), **Device frame** (plain monitor or phone), **Split** (one shot in two rungs, Final against Clay) and **End card** (logo, tagline, repo line). The inspector edits the look, text, a blue word, start and length, position (or the callout's box), type and accent colours, the window clips with their rung and in-frame, and sounds on or off. Drag a graphic in the viewer to move it; drag or trim its block on V3 to retime it. The lane appears with the first graphic.
- **One timing, two painters:** `stateAt` (browser preview) and `state_at` ([soundstage/cut/gfx.py](soundstage/cut/gfx.py), the render) give the same draw ops for every frame; tests/js/fixtures/gfx_states.json pins 336 frames and both suites check it. The easing matches the spec's reference tables.
- **Render:** with graphics, the picture pass goes to a lossless FFV1 file, Pillow paints each frame (raqm kerning on the pod; motion blur over a half-frame shutter with 4 to 16 sub-frames on moving frames), then the V2 titles and the mix go on as before ([soundstage/cut/render.py](soundstage/cut/render.py)).
- **Sounds:** each event gets its designed sound from §13 (tick, swap ticks, sub thump, whooshes in and out, a riser that ends as the zoom lands, ping, click, air pass, puff, the F add9 swell), placed on A2 at the event's frame and level, panned with the element, and re-laid on every edit so they never stack. They are synthesised with numpy by [soundstage/cut/gfx_sfx.py](soundstage/cut/gfx_sfx.py) (no samples, no downloads, no credits; builds in about 2 s on the pod) into output/soundstage_sfx/gfx/ with a MANIFEST.json; [tools/qa/gfx_sfx_qc.py](tools/qa/gfx_sfx_qc.py) is the classifier check.
- **Director:** `cut.graphic`, `cut.update_graphic`, `cut.remove_graphic`. Docs: [docs/cut.md](docs/cut.md), [docs/TUTORIAL.md](docs/TUTORIAL.md).
- **Tests:** tests/js/cut_gfx.test.mjs (5), tests/test_cut_gfx.py (8, including a render that checks pixels for the title, PiP, split, callout ring and end card). Headless in the App on the Mac mini (no GPU jobs): five graphics added from the menu, a line typed in the inspector, the PiP dragged in the viewer (share/Commercial/graphics/).

**05:55 PST · ④ Frame: Lock to the stage depth (Off · Light · Strict), tested against ghost objects**
- **Why:** frames still come out with things the stage does not have, or the wrong size (the riders' horses 3x too big, a man walking between them who is not in the scene). The stage renders an exact depth pass; the question was whether holding the image model to it stops that.
- **What** ([soundstage/router/adapters.py](soundstage/router/adapters.py), [soundstage/look/frame_lock.py](soundstage/look/frame_lock.py), [web/pipeline/pipeline.mjs](web/pipeline/pipeline.mjs)): under Follow the clay, **Lock to the stage depth**. **Light**: FLUX 3 Klein paints the frame, then Krea 2 Turbo + the Krea2 depth-control LoRA repaint it at 0.5 on the depth pass (keeps your cast pictures). **Strict**: Krea 2 Turbo paints the frame itself from the blurred Color pass at Follow the clay, held to the depth pass (no cast pictures: the identity-edit patch broke the hold). **Off** stays the default. Each pick records its lock, so frames picked before come back exactly (frame_lock VERSION 3). A frame whose actors miss their marks suggests Strict. Models already on the pod; nothing downloaded.
- **Numbers** (same stage, prompt and seed; silhouette match with the clay, Off / Light / Strict): riders' horses 15% / 16% / **67%**; car chase hero car **82%** / 86% / 20% (police car 0% / 0% / 63%); two-person street (both people) **93%** / 79% / 71%. Light added four far pedestrians to the street and Strict a person in the foreground, so neither became the default. Realism judge: street 0 / 7 / 7 (Klein's people wore mannequin clothes), car 0 / 1 / 1. The comparison sheets are in the project's share/OneClick/C folder.

**05:50 PST · Picking a shot puts Camera view on that shot's camera, and a click picks the rider you see**
- **The bug (found by the readiness check):** after a shot was picked, Camera view kept the camera of the last setup in the strip (1P *Ezra alone*) while the label named the chosen shot. On *1G Mara close single* the view showed Ezra, so clicking the rider selected Ezra and *Real performance…* opened on him. Clicking **Camera view** fixed it until the next pick.
- **The cause:** the strip's clay thumbnails, the Cut editor's previews, ④'s shot rows and the script view rendered other setups by flipping `state.active_shot` around `stage.renderFrameTo`. `renderFrameTo` put the viewport back while the flip was still in place, so the shot camera stayed on the last setup it drew, and picking used that camera.
- **The fix** ([web/stage/stage.mjs](web/stage/stage.mjs)): `renderFrameTo(mode, frame, { shot })` renders another setup and puts the active shot back *before* it restores the viewport. Every caller passes `{ shot }` now ([web/cut/strip.mjs](web/cut/strip.mjs), [web/soundstage.js](web/soundstage.js)). The stage also remembers which shot its camera was posed for: the viewport, the click pick, the gizmo, the performance fit's box and the pose pass re-pose it on the active shot if anything flipped it ([web/performance/performance.mjs](web/performance/performance.mjs), [web/stage/posepass.mjs](web/stage/posepass.mjs)), and a flip left by an older caller is redrawn right after that call. A live preview camera (phone, preset hover) is left alone.
- **Tests:** tests/js/shotcam_follow.test.mjs (7): the strip's own thumbnail pass over four setups, picking 1G, 1H, 1G, 1P, 1G with a click at the centre of Camera view selecting that shot's rider each time, the old flip pattern, Free view and back, and a live preview. Five of the seven fail on the code before this fix. Headless on the pod with *Last_Light_on_Route_9*: every strip pick lands on its own camera (0.000 m off), and a click on Mara in 1G selects Mara.

**04:40 PST · The Quickstart, tested by two fresh-eyes runs: a first shot in ten minutes, then a one-page scene; and four first-run snags fixed**
- **New:** [docs/QUICKSTART.md](docs/QUICKSTART.md), linked from the README and the tutorial: four rules first (never ComfyUI's Run, one shot at a time, Esc only to close an open panel, read a yellow button), a ten-step first shot on the demo, the *Last Light at the Pump* scene through all seven steps, and the rough edges. Every step was checked against the live App by two headless testers with no GPU jobs.

**03:30 PST · ✦ One-click, track B: Seamless loop with a seam meter, Reshoot from a new angle, Open in ComfyUI, Film a move on the phone**
- **Flaws first (measured on the pod, frame by frame):**

On a canoe on a lake (89 frames), the last latent block snapped toward the picture 3 frames from the end. The meter read **Visible seam · 8.3x (jump at frame 86)**. A 1 s bridge render brought the paddlers back to their pose, but not the water: the join measured 17x, so the fixer kept the original. On a street frame from a crash shot, LTX drove the car around and through the crates (it teleports between frames): **Visible seam · 2.5x**, and it fails the Hollywood bar on content alone. The meter and the fixer work; the loop render itself is not good enough yet.
  - **Reshoot with a depth guide failed the realism bar.** On the riders (10° orbit), it kept the blocking and the gallop. But both riders' heads became blown hair masses, the hat was gone, a ghost woman stood behind a horse, the edge reveal became a brown wall, and the last second melted. The guide is now Canny edges plus auto-framing (the reveal pushed out of the frame, 1.25x). The re-render keeps the run, the mount and the gallop in step with the original, with faces, hats and reins, and no edge wall. **Still not Hollywood:** the woman's red shirt drifts to brown and she gains a hat mid-clip, and the last half second, where the riders leave frame, dissolves into a dust blob. It needs a likeness ref (her frame-1 crop) and a tail cut before it ships as a take you'd use.
  - The pod's LTX-2 19B camera LoRAs load on 2.5 22B (480 of 480 layers match), but their effect is unproven (no control render).
  - The new routes need a server restart. Until then the cards say "this server needs a restart".
- **Seamless loop** ([soundstage/loop/](soundstage/loop/), [web/oneclick/loop.mjs](web/oneclick/loop.mjs), [docs/loop.md](docs/loop.md)):
  - LTX-2.5 with the picture fixed at frame 0 and guided at the last index.
  - A **CPU seam meter** compares the wrap step against the clip's own median step (pixel, edge SSIM, LK flow, flow jerk). It badges *Seamless* / *Visible seam · N×* with the reason: a held frame, a jump at frame N, or the metric outside the clip's range.
  - A **fixer** tries, in order, dropping the duplicate last frame, a trim to the best frame pair, then one bridge render from the last clean frame back to frame 1, then a reseed, reporting before → after. Never a crossfade.
  - Cards: Seamless loop, Seam meter (no GPU), Fix a loop seam.
- **Reshoot from a new angle** ([soundstage/reshoot/](soundstage/reshoot/), [web/oneclick/reshoot.mjs](web/oneclick/reshoot.mjs), [docs/reshoot.md](docs/reshoot.md)):
  - Depth Anything 3 runs on every frame of the take (cached per take). The camera is re-aimed relative to the original on every frame, capped at ±15° orbit, ±8° rise, 25% dolly and 0.7-1.6x lens.
  - Each frame is splatted into the new view. Sky goes to infinity, cracks close from the near side and reveals fill from the far side.
  - Then LTX-2.5 union control (Recast's engine) with the take's own sound. The take says how much of the view was invented.
- **Open in ComfyUI / Download the graph** ([soundstage/handoff/](soundstage/handoff/), [web/oneclick/opencomfy.mjs](web/oneclick/opencomfy.mjs)): the exact graph inside any take (the workflow tag, else the API prompt) opens in a **new tab**, framed. Headless check: the App's tab still holds its 54 nodes and the stage afterwards. No GPU.
- **Film a move** ([web/vcam/film.mjs](web/vcam/film.mjs), [soundstage/vcam.py](soundstage/vcam.py), [web/oneclick/handoff.mjs](web/oneclick/handoff.mjs), [docs/handoff.md](docs/handoff.md)):
  - The phone viewfinder records a real clip (https only) and sends it with the QR's clip code. The server caps uploads at 300 MB and converts the clip to MP4 in `input/soundstage_private/phone/`.
  - The clip lands as Capture motion, Real performance (the dialog opens with it loaded) or Footage (Ripple).
  - The bridge lets only the clip upload through.
- **MCP:** `soundstage_loop`, `soundstage_reshoot` and `soundstage_take_workflow` run server-side (proposed first, `confirm_gpu` to render).
- **Tests:** tests/test_loop_graphs.py, test_loop_seam.py, test_loop_fix.py, test_reshoot.py and test_handoff.py; tests/js/loop, reshoot and handoff. Full suites on the rebased tree: pytest 1200 passed, 25 skipped; node 864 passed.
- **GPU used:** 8 jobs: loop ×2, bridge, reshoot depth, reshoot ×3 (depth guide, edge guide, and one that failed at the audio encode because the riders take has no sound; it now renders silent), and the camera-LoRA load test. Draft renders: loop 55-206 s, bridge 190 s, reshoot 58-197 s, plus a CPU warp of 1.5-4 min on the busy pod.
- **12:45 PST review fix ([web/oneclick/loop.mjs](web/oneclick/loop.mjs), and the same in the MCP `soundstage_loop`, [tools/soundstage_mcp.py](tools/soundstage_mcp.py)):** the fixer kept the sealed loop (the repeated last frame dropped) only when re-encoding happened to lower the seam number, and skipped sealing altogether when the render already read Seamless. The meter measures the wrap without the repeat, so the badge described a file the take did not hold: it could play a held frame at the wrap. Sealing is now kept whenever a frame was dropped, also on a Seamless render. tests/js/loop.test.mjs (+2; both fail on the old fixer) and tests/test_mcp_loop.py (2; the Seamless case fails on the old MCP tool).

**01:20 PST · Boat verification: steering the right way, motor trim that trims, people-sized paddle forces, a flat-sailed Laser, paddles below the eyes**
- **Flaws found and fixed** ([web/stage/boatsim.mjs](web/stage/boatsim.mjs)): the kayak's sweeps and the rower's oars steered the **wrong way** (the raft's paddles too, hidden under the guide's strokes); motor trim was **inverted** (trimming out pushed the bow down, and the cowl tilted the wrong way); a raft starting slower than the river took **860 N** strokes per paddler and lurched; a speedboat at 30 kn hard over turned a **130 m** circle (no gearcase lift) while the inboard fishing boat spun at 66°/s; the Laser sailor hiked at 0.75 m with no rig inertia or sail roll damping, so the dinghy rolled ±25–35°.
- **Now:** steer + turns every craft left; a raft crew back-paddles the inside to turn (~7°/s); trim out lifts the bow ~1° more at the hump; blade force is capped at a strong paddler's ~320 N and the raft starts riding the current; the speedboat carves a ~35 m circle at 30 kn banked 8° in, the fishing boat ~29 m; the dinghy sails at ~11° of heel in 13 kn with the sailor out at 1.05 m and the sheet eased past 7°.
- **Paddles** ([web/stage/watercraft.mjs](web/stage/watercraft.mjs)): the top hand stays at chin to eye height of the rider (measured on the clay rig in the live App: it used to sit a head-height overhead), the hands stack over a buried blade mid-drive, and the blade slices in and out on one continuous loop (it popped 36 cm between drive and recovery). The outboard cowl is a cowl, not a ball. Lake and River rapids get real ground and water colours in the Color pass ([web/stage/setlook.mjs](web/stage/setlook.mjs), new on main since the boats landed).
- **Presets fit the shot** ([web/presets/boatpresets.mjs](web/presets/boatpresets.mjs)): in a 4–5 s shot the speedboat now levels off on the plane and starts its carve, and the tack and the raft's ferry come early (they were scripted at 5–6.5 s, past the end of the shot).
- **Drive mode** ([web/stage/drive.mjs](web/stage/drive.mjs), [web/ui/drivehud.mjs](web/ui/drivehud.mjs)): checked in the live App, headless: the speedboat climbs the hump at 2 s, planes by 4–5 s, turns and banks the way you steer; the canoe paddles at ~2 m/s and records its strokes. Motor trim set with Space / Q now stays where you put it (it snapped back to auto), and the help line and HUD give each boat's own controls.
- **Still open:** canoe and rowboat turns are slow (2–4°/s); the log raft's pole barely steers; no capsize, swamping or rock collisions (the raft passes through boulders); the wake is drawn, not simulated; the surface field is separate from the reef's water.mjs; no new GPU Draft was made for this round. Tests: 8 new in [tests/js/boats.test.mjs](tests/js/boats.test.mjs) (steering sign and rate, trim, blade force, raft start, Laser heel, paddle pose, preset beats, live Drive).

**01:00 PST · Monday's recording blockers: the cast's voices match, the Real performance dialog shows the queue, stays on its shot and keeps the tail; ④ frames survive a restart**
- **Why:** the prep for Mike's session left the Real performance dialog offering "Convert to Ezra (EZRA)" while the saved voices are EZRA_HALE and MARA_VOSS (GET /soundstage/voices answers 404 until the server restarts, and the guess was the cue name), so Read failed. A job waiting behind other jobs on the shared card showed only "Reading… 1167 s"; Render went to whatever shot was active, not the one the dialog was opened on; the capture dropped up to 0.29 s off the clip's end; and a chosen ④ frame lived only in temp/, which a restart empties.
- **Voices match the cast** ([web/performance/performance.mjs](web/performance/performance.mjs), [soundstage/audio/voices.py](soundstage/audio/voices.py)):
  - `matchVoice`: an exact name or alias ("EZRA HALE" → EZRA_HALE), the voice saved for that character, or the one voice whose name starts with the first name ("Ezra" → EZRA_HALE; two Ezras → no guess).
  - Before the restart (no voice list), the dialog reads the full name from the script ("EZRA HALE (60s…" in the action → EZRA_HALE) and the cast entry's `aliases` / `full_name`, instead of offering the cue name. A stale cast voice ("EZRA") heals to the match once the list is there.
  - The server resolves the same way (`resolve_name`): "Ezra", "EZRA" or "ezra hale" load EZRA_HALE. Live after the next restart.
- **Queue state:** `followPrompt` follows our prompt by websocket (execution_start / executing carry its id) and, when the server has it, the light queue summary for "N jobs ahead". The read and every render say "Waiting in the queue (3 jobs ahead): the GPU is shared…" until the job starts, then "Reading… 41 s". No full /queue or /history polling.
- **Pinned shot:** the dialog belongs to the shot it was opened on (its title says so). Apply, Render and Ripple go back to that shot first if the strip moved on, so the fit, the motion prompt and the take all land on it.
- **The tail:** Load Clip has `tail = "hold"` ([soundstage/motion/timing.py](soundstage/motion/timing.py), [soundstage/nodes_motion.py](soundstage/nodes_motion.py)): it pads up to the next 8k+1 frames with the last frame and silence instead of dropping up to 7 frames. Performance captures use it ([soundstage/performance/graphs.py](soundstage/performance/graphs.py)); the dialog sends it only when the server's Load Clip lists it. Until then the dialog says how much was dropped ("the last 0.33 s are dropped… re-cut with ½ s after the last word").
- **④ frames out of temp/** ([web/pipeline/pipeline.mjs](web/pipeline/pipeline.mjs)): a chosen frame is copied once into input/soundstage_frames/ and the chosen record (and its tile) point at the copy; model and seed stay for Motion.
- **Tests:** tests/js/performance.test.mjs (+4: name/alias matching, the pre-restart choice, queue state with the summary and an early start, tail notes and frame counts), tests/test_audio_clip.py (voice resolution), tests/test_motion_graphs.py (tail hold), the Load Clip snapshot. JS 796, Python 1128 green (after rebasing on a4e33dc).
- **Still open:** the server half (voice resolution, tail hold, /soundstage/voices, the queue summary) is live only after the pod restarts; the dialog is correct without it. A stale shot is restored by switching the strip back, which moves the view.

**00:55 PST · Behind the README media: real set colours in Color, lit dust, real car words, smoke and riders kept apart**

- **Every set's real colour in the Color pass** ([web/stage/setlook.mjs](web/stage/setlook.mjs)): the floor, the backdrop and the set dressing of all 18 sets are now coloured: desert sand (the same sand its dust is made of, from particles.mjs), grass and infield clay, asphalt, brick, leaf litter, beach sand, sea and tank water, and fresh snow on every outdoor set when it snows. Open land (desert, beach, field, forest, sea) now runs out to the horizon instead of stopping 40 m out. Clay, Depth and the ID matte are unchanged (they override every material).
- **Dust and smoke as lit volumes** ([web/stage/secondary.mjs](web/stage/secondary.mjs)): each puff is shaded as a soft ball lit by the set's sun (wrapped), sky and ground light, darker in its thick core and glowing at its thin edges when backlit (forward scatter). Street lamps and headlights light the smoke at night, and a floor keeps a plume from sinking into the dark. The colour was also a bug: the sRGB sand was used as linear, so sand dust rendered near-white. Puffs now sit on the ground instead of being sliced flat by it.

Every on-screen steer (path, keyed, driven live, the sim's 52° stunt lock) is now clamped to `steer_lock` in [library.mjs](web/stage/library.mjs). A rolled or pitched body is lifted by however far its lowest arch lip would sink into its tyre, so the stunt lean pivots on the outside wheels instead of dropping the fenders through them.
- **The prompts** ([soundstage/look/identity.py](soundstage/look/identity.py), [stunts.py](soundstage/look/stunts.py)):
  - Each car is named as the body style its model really is, with no brand names: the sports car is "a modern two-door fastback sports coupé with a long low hood, a short rear deck…", and car.glb (the library's "hatchback") is a four-door sedan. It always has "a blank plain white license plate with no lettering, and no badges or logos".
  - Tyre smoke is said in every beat it is in, as thick as the stunt sim bakes it: when it starts, "thick, billowing clouds of white tyre smoke and spray pour off all four tyres", the car "bursting out of its own cloud", and the smoke "still hanging in the air at the end of the shot".
  - With two or more people, each one's hat or hair and clothes are said by name, from the Look description and the appearance picker: "the man wears … a cowboy hat, his short dark hair under the hat; the woman … bareheaded, her long dark hair down her back. Each rider keeps their own hat, hair and clothes from the first frame to the last".
- **Checked in the App** (Mac mini, headless Metal, origin/main against this change, the round-1 western and J-turn scenes): the desert is sand-coloured to the horizon, the J-turn's front wheel sits inside its arch at f64, and the tyre smoke reads as soft grey plumes instead of white cut-outs.
- **Tests:** new tests/js/mediafix.test.mjs (every set's colour and reach, the floor colour on the stage, snow cover, lit dust in linear colour, the steering lock re-measured from each GLB, the 52° clamp, the arch lift) and tests/test_mediafix_words.py (body words with no brands, the blank plate, smoke beats from the baked keys, per-person anchors).

**00:15 PST · Boats: speedboats, a fishing boat, sailing dinghy and yacht, canoe, kayak, rowboat, whitewater and log rafts, on real waves**
- **Nine watercraft** with hulls lofted from the same stations their physics floats on ([web/stage/boatmesh.mjs](web/stage/boatmesh.mjs)), and Parts for every control: throttle, motor trim, rudder, sheet, boom, paddling effort, nav / anchor / deck lights.
- **Boat physics** ([web/stage/boatsim.mjs](web/stage/boatsim.mjs)): buoyancy over ~100 hull columns on the wave field (heave, pitch, roll), Savitsky planing lift with transom ventilation and the hull's own wave (the bow climbs over the hump, then levels as it planes: 5–7° at the hump, ~1.5–3° planing), sails from the apparent wind with stall, luffing, cos² heel loss, a keel foil and the crew hiking, and paddle / oar strokes whose blade slip surges the hull each catch, with the people's arms on the paddle (two-bone IK) in sync.
- **The water** ([web/stage/waves.mjs](web/stage/waves.mjs), [web/stage/watercraft.mjs](web/stage/watercraft.mjs)): a seeded JONSWAP wave field scaled to the sea state (glassy to rough; whitewater standing waves), drawn in the clay and Color guides to the horizon; Kelvin wake ribbons, bow spray, prop wash and paddle drips through the shared particles. New sets: **Lake** and **River rapids**. Drive mode drives boats on the same physics and records the strokes and throttle.
- **Presets:** Speedboat run, Sailing tack, Canoe on a calm lake, Whitewater raft. Prompts say what boats do (planing, sailing heeled, paddling in time) and lock the water, not a "deserted street".
- **Verified** (share/Boats): LTX Drafts of the speedboat run and the canoe. Flaws: the speedboat holds one raised trim (Klein's frame 1 is already running, so the hole shot is lost) and its crew grows from 2 to 3–4; the canoe gets a third paddler by the dock and swings the paddles overhead; the dinghy rolls ±25–30° through a tack; no capsize or rock collisions; the surface field is separate from the reef's optics waves (water.mjs). Tests: [tests/js/boats.test.mjs](tests/js/boats.test.mjs) (buoyancy equilibrium, righting, planing curve, heel against wind, the tack, stroke sync, determinism, Hs, hulls, wakes, presets), [tests/test_boat_words.py](tests/test_boat_words.py).

## 2026-11-01

**23:20 PST · Swim verification: an exhale now carries a real breath of gas, so the bubble bursts read**
- **Why:** checked frame by frame against real dive footage, each exhale let out about 0.03 L of gas, some 50 times less than a real breath. The bursts were a few specks of 1-2 px.
- **Fix** ([web/stage/swim.mjs](web/stage/swim.mjs)): each exhale carries 1-2.5 L. Most of it leaves in the first gush as about twenty big wobbling caps (3-6.5 cm), with a fizz of millimetre bubbles around and after them. The reef draws up to 3200 bubbles ([web/stage/sets/underwater.mjs](web/stage/sets/underwater.mjs)).
- **Tests:** new test in tests/js/swim.test.mjs. It checks the volume per breath (Boyle-corrected back to the regulator's depth) and at least ten big caps, over four seeds; it fails on the old code with 0.03 L.
- **Still open:** caustic lines are spaced 1.3-1.9 m apart on the sand (real reef caustics are finer); a low diver path goes straight through coral; the Hollywood scene's keys ask for a 1.5 m/s sprint.

**23:10 PST · Swimming and water: real strokes, the regulator in the mouth, bubbles in bursts, caustics and surge**
- **Why:** the reef's Hollywood take had the regulator hanging below the mouth, a diver gliding with hardly a kick and stiff water. The guide had no dive kit at all, the "swim" was a fixed pose that bobbed, and nothing in either prompt said where the regulator goes.
- **Strokes from physics** ([web/stage/swim.mjs](web/stage/swim.mjs)):
  - **Flutter kick.** The cadence comes from the Strouhal number (St = f·A/v ≈ 0.6 for a finned diver): a 0.5 m/s cruise is a 0.7 Hz kick with a 0.43 m fin sweep, always within 0.5-1 Hz.
  - **Other underwater styles.** Frog kick, hover, and the legacy `stroke`.
  - **At the surface.** Freestyle (37° body roll, high-elbow catch, high recovery, a six-beat kick, breathing to the side), breaststroke and treading water.
  - **Surge.** Each style's thrust pulses against v² drag with the entrained water's mass. A frog kick surges 0.29-0.52 m/s and coasts, and breaststroke 0.52-1.08 m/s; a flutter kick is steady.
  - **Trim and buoyancy.** Level trim, pitched along a climb and banked into turns. Each breath (3-5 s, per diver) lifts the diver a few cm, about 0.9 s late.
- **The regulator is in the mouth.** New scuba kit: mask, a regulator whose mouthpiece passes through the lips (tested), the hose over the right shoulder to the first stage (rebuilt every frame), tank, jacket, octopus, weight belt and fins.
- **Secondary motion.** The fin blades bend against their own velocity through the water and snap through at each reversal. Strap tails and long hair stream back and drift.
- **The prompts say it** ([soundstage/look/swim.py](soundstage/look/swim.py)): "the regulator's mouthpiece held in his mouth with its black hose looping over his right shoulder…", the kick, and "a burst of silver bubbles every few seconds". The still's negative now includes "regulator hanging below the chin".
- **Bubbles.** Each exhale is a burst from the exhaust tee: big cap bubbles first, then a trickle. They rise at their size's speed (Davies-Taylor for caps), grow by Boyle's law (×1.26 over 10 m), wobble and drift with the current, and are drawn at their true size as rim-lit rings.
- **Water** ([web/stage/water.mjs](web/stage/water.mjs)):
  - **One wave field** moves the reef's surface (displaced, with Snell's 48.6° window from below). It also focuses the caustics in Color (1/|det J| of the refraction map, the same maths on the CPU and in GLSL) on the sand, coral, kelp and diver, and flickers the light shafts.
  - **The light shafts** now fall along the refracted sun.
  - **Surge.** A long swell's surge (0.17 m/s at 8 m) sways the kelp, with the wave climbing the blade. The sea fans and sea grass are now a swaying mesh, so they move in Clay too.
  - **Particles.** The marine snow, bubbles and stirred sand drift with the current.
  - **Hard coral stays rigid**; staghorn colonies now grow toward the light, with irregular forks, knobs and pale tips.
- **Stirred sand.** Fins kicking down within 1.2 m of the bottom feed the shared particle system (`seabed_silt`: it hangs 5-9 s and never sinks through the seabed).
- **Wakes.** Surface swimmers leave foam: bow wave, kick, hand entries and the Kelvin wedge.
- **New Action preset: Diver swim.**
- **Judged frame by frame** (App passes on the pod, Metal, every 4th frame; share/Swim_Water/): the diver now lies level and kicks visibly every frame, the tank, jacket and hose read in Clay, caustics move on the sand in Color, and bubbles leave the head and trail behind the diver. Open-water freestyle shows the roll and the arm recovery over the water.
- **One Draft through the App** (the Hollywood reef scene, Draft quality, queued by the App's own queuePrompt; share/Swim_Water/draft/). The regulator sits in the mouth, the mask and hose read, and the diver flutter-kicks on every frame with broad yellow fins instead of gliding. But it looks like a **CG animation, not photographed water**: the Draft copies the Color guide's flat clay shading. The pod still ran the old prompt compile (no restart), so the swim words went in through the Look description by hand.
- **Still wrong:**
  - The fin blades are seen edge-on from the side and read as thin lines; the foot pockets look boxy.
  - The mask hardly reads at this size.
  - Stirred sand is the colour of the sand, so it barely shows (26 puffs at frame 48, hard to see).
  - Small bubbles are 1-2 px at 960 px wide.
  - The open-water surface is still the old flat grey plane, so the foam looks pasted on.
- Docs: [docs/swim.md](docs/swim.md). Tests: [tests/js/swim.test.mjs](tests/js/swim.test.mjs) (21), [tests/test_swim_words.py](tests/test_swim_words.py) (4).

**23:10 PST · Act every part yourself: Voice: keep mine / convert to the character, in Real performance**
- **One actor, every voice.** The Real performance dialog has a **Voice** choice: *Keep my voice*, *Convert to Mara Voss (MARA_VOSS)*, or any other saved Character Voice. The clip's sound goes through Chatterbox VC (local, free, no new weights) into the character's voice before the performance is saved, so the face, the lips and the converted voice keep the performer's timing, breaths and pauses ([web/performance/performance.mjs](web/performance/performance.mjs)).
- **The voice lives on the cast entry.** The voice you pick is stored as `voice` on the ② Cast entry and shown on its card, so the next take of that character starts on it ([web/cast/cast.mjs](web/cast/cast.mjs)). `GET /soundstage/voices` lists the saved voices: names, characters and owners, no paths ([soundstage/routes.py](soundstage/routes.py)).
- **A small job.** It is one more node (id 10) between the clip's sound and Save Performance, about 840 bytes in all, and the dialog waits on its own `/history/<id>`. It ran end to end on :8189: the length matched to the sample, the median pitch moved from 112 Hz to 171 Hz, and the envelope lag was 0.
- **Long takes in pieces, pauses kept silent.** A take over 20 s is converted in pieces cut in the middle of its pauses. On a 55 s pass, Ezra's similarity rose from 0.86 to 0.89. A pause guard stops the mumbles Chatterbox VC can leave in long silences (2.7 s at -35 dBFS, measured). A clip without sound passes through ([soundstage/audio/vcchunk.py](soundstage/audio/vcchunk.py), [soundstage/audio/tts.py](soundstage/audio/tts.py)).
- **Measured on synthetic stand-ins** (full bench and WAVs in `share/Recording_Session_Mon/voice/`). Word onsets moved by a median 0-20 ms. Similarity to the target was 0.89-0.92 for Ezra and 0.89-0.95 for Mara. A +3 to +6 semitone pre-shift for a man playing a woman did not help, and a formant shift cost words, so there is none. **Known risk:** on a source Chatterbox did not make, Whisper-base heard 39-51% of Ezra's words wrong. Listen to every converted Ezra line.
- Tests: [tests/test_voice_convert.py](tests/test_voice_convert.py), [tests/js/performance.test.mjs](tests/js/performance.test.mjs). Docs: [docs/performance.md](docs/performance.md#voice-keep-mine--convert-to-the-character), [docs/audio.md](docs/audio.md#voice-change-directed-performances). **Needs a ComfyUI restart** for the chunking, the pause guard and the voices list. Until then, clips of 20 s or less convert whole.

**23:05 PST · Queue-bloat fix reviewed: Export (API) carries the whole stage again, about 30x less work per queue on the event loop**
- **Export (API) was not self-contained.** The 3D Stage widget swapped a big stage for a reference inside `serializeValue`, which is also what ComfyUI's Export (API) reads, so a downloaded API workflow carried only `{"stage_ref": …}`. The swap now happens only when a prompt is queued, in the App's `api.queuePrompt` wrapper (`stageInputsByRef`, [web/ui/queue.mjs](web/ui/queue.mjs)). Export (API) and the saved UI workflow carry the scene inline. Queued prompts, the history, and the PNG and MP4 metadata of a render carry the reference; the scene is in the rendering server's `input/soundstage_stages/<sha256>.json` and `GET /soundstage/stage_store/<sha256>` returns it.
- **Less work on the event loop per queue.** For an App render of a 2.1 MB stage, the on-prompt handlers took ~220 ms. They parsed the workflow copy twice and read the stored stage back to rebuild the MP4 recipe. Now they take ~7 ms: the workflow copy is hashed once and never parsed, and the recipe points at the same stored stage with the shot read from the reference. A stage sent inline (the stock UI, an old script) costs one parse.
- **Opening a workflow whose set this server doesn't have** now says so on the stage instead of quietly showing the default set (before, only the browser console said so).
- **Load test** ([tests/test_queue_load.py](tests/test_queue_load.py)): 20 crate-crash renders posted to a stub ComfyUI (ComfyUI's on-prompt order, the deep-copying `GET /queue`). Without the store, one `GET /queue` is 174 MB and takes 6.3 s. With it, `GET /queue` is 3.6 MB and takes 0.28 s, and `GET /soundstage/queue_summary` is 3.2 KB with a median of 0.39 ms. Ten summary polls plus one queue take 98 ms.
- Still open: a ComfyUI restart on the pod is needed for the routes and handlers. The workflow copy (~150-180 KB per item) still makes `GET /queue` megabytes. `ss_script.scene_stages` is not slimmed. The store is never cleaned up.

**22:57 PST · No menu is ever cut off: one pop-over layer for every menu, drop-down and context menu**
- **The bug:** at 1600x1000 with Tracks open, the Cut strip's + Shot menu opened upward inside the scrolling box under the canvas, so its top 120 px (Copy of the current camera, Master, Wide) could not be seen or clicked. The same kind of problem affected other menus. At 1024 wide, the scene picker's chips ran past the window. With the Director open, the Look palette sat under the Director panel. In Takes, Arrange ▾ and the Cut editor's right-click menu could be hidden by the view's own CSS. The phone viewfinder's QR card could run off a short window and stayed open until its × was clicked.
- **The fix, at the root:** [web/ui/popover.mjs](web/ui/popover.mjs) renders every menu in one top-level layer (`document.body`, `position:fixed`). It flips to the side of its anchor that has room, is clamped to the window, and scrolls inside when it is taller than the window. Esc and a click outside close it; the arrow keys, Home and End move between its items. It keeps the App's look: its theme classes (not the view-mode ones, which hide other children) and CSS variables. These now use it: the Cut strip's + Shot, clip … and right-click menus; the top row's scene picker and Tools ▾; the Look palette; the Shots row's scene switcher; Takes › Arrange ▾; the Cut editor's right-click menu; the phone viewfinder QR; the take-check pop-over; and the placement of the review pop-over and the lined script's hover card. Esc now closes the ④/⑤ settings pop-overs (Sound & music, Style…) and the Look palette from anywhere, without leaving the full workspace.
- **Tests:** placement maths ([tests/js/popover.test.mjs](tests/js/popover.test.mjs), including a grid of anchors and sizes in five window sizes) and a guard that every `role=menu` goes through the layer ([tests/js/popover_guard.test.mjs](tests/js/popover_guard.test.mjs)). Headless sweep: [tools/qa/popover_sweep.mjs](tools/qa/popover_sweep.mjs) opens 18 menus and pop-overs with a real click, at 1024x640, 1280x800, 1600x1000 and 1920x1080, with Tracks, Presets, Physics, Parts and Director open in turn. Each must lie fully in the window with every item clickable, close on Esc, and leave the layer empty. Result: 292 checks, 0 menu failures. Before the fix, 13 of 29 checks failed at 1600x1000 alone.
- Still open: at 1024 and 1280 wide, the Physics panel covers the shot strip's clips, so their menus cannot be reached while it is open (the sweep lists this as UNDER PANEL). At 1024 wide, the top row runs past the window, so Director, Guide and Done cannot be seen. Coach tips were not tested because the demo scene has none.

**22:15 PST · The pod freeze fixed at the root: the stage goes by reference, nobody polls the full queue**
- **What froze the pod at 19:18:** every render queued from the App carried the whole 3D stage up to four times: the 3D Stage node's input, the workflow copy in its metadata (twice: `widgets_values` and `widgets_values_named`), and the MP4 recipe tag. ComfyUI also writes the input into the prompt's `is_changed` once it runs. A busy scene is megabytes: the crate crash's fracture bakes made one queue item 3.3 MB. ComfyUI's `GET /queue` and `GET /history` encode every queued and finished prompt in full. With 20 crate-crash renders queued, one `GET /queue` was 65 MB and 0.65 s of JSON encoding on the event loop's only thread. App pages and test browsers polled it, so the server stopped answering.
- **The stage by reference.** A stage over 16 KB is stored once, content-addressed, in ComfyUI's `input/soundstage_stages/<sha256>.json`. The prompt carries only `{"stage_ref": …, "bytes", "frames", "shot_id"}`, and the 3D Stage node loads it by hash. An on-prompt handler does the same for every client (old saved workflows, scripts, the stock UI) and for the workflow copy and the recipe tag. Inline JSON keeps working. A workflow or MP4 that carries a reference loads its scene back from this server's store ([soundstage/stagestore.py](soundstage/stagestore.py), [web/ui/queue.mjs](web/ui/queue.mjs)).
- **Measured on the real crate-crash prompt from the pod:** the API prompt went from 1,497 KB to 3.2 KB, and the whole queue item from 3,269 KB to 153 KB. Most of what is left is the App graph's own workflow copy. A real 90-node App render prompt went from 89 KB to 21 KB. `GET /queue` with the same 20 renders queued went from 65.4 MB / 649 ms to 3.05 MB / 36 ms.
- **`GET /soundstage/queue_summary`** returns running and pending counts plus prompt ids, client ids and short labels ("Final · Chase cam"), never a payload. Use `?ids=` for your own prompts. Over 20 pre-fix crate-crash items it answers in 2.3 KB, with a median of 0.39 ms ([soundstage/queuewatch.py](soundstage/queuewatch.py)).
- **No App, tool or QA code fetches the full `/queue` or `/history` any more.** The Stop button, Stop all, the object designer, the scene agent, the Director, Pitch Mode's sketch jobs, the 3D asset maker, the live agent harness and the QA driver all use the summary. A tool's own result comes from `/history/{prompt_id}` or the websocket events. A test fails if any code fetches the full endpoints again. Tools and agents: see [tools/qa/README.md](tools/qa/README.md#watching-the-queue) and [docs/mcp.md](docs/mcp.md).
- Needs a ComfyUI restart on the pod for the new routes and the on-prompt handler. Until then the App falls back to sending the stage inline, as before. Tests: [tests/test_queue_light.py](tests/test_queue_light.py), [tests/js/queue.test.mjs](tests/js/queue.test.mjs).

- **The object bar now shows in Camera view** (with or without the Physics panel open). On a tracking or crane shot the camera moves with the frame before the renderer refreshes its matrices, so the bar was placed with last frame's camera: the selected car "went behind the camera" and the bar hid. - **Night prompts no longer add a sun.** "night, cool moonlight, low sun 0° above the horizon …, 4100K warm sunlight": the moon's key light was read as a sun. A Milestone 26 proof Draft of the night chase showed the sunset glow it caused on the horizon.
- **Parts show the real values when you switch objects.** Selecting a second untouched object (a crate, then a car) left its rows unset: door sliders sat mid-track as if half open, the numbers blank. Long Parts labels ("Passenger door", "Rear right door", "Head up / down") wrap instead of ending in "…".
- **Pitch Mode with a denied mic** says so once and stops listening. The browser's speech recognition restarted itself after every error, so the Director filled with "Speech recognition stopped (not-allowed)" a dozen times a second.
